[Previous
Version](section-8.2.2-20150211.html "Link to previous version of section 8.2.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other skin data from animals

**[[8.2.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.2.3 .sectionLabel}** A substance for
which there are animal data on dermal exposure, acquired from a
scientifically validated method, that have not been purposely generated
and that demonstrate that the substance is skin-corrosive or
skin-irritant is classified, respectively, in the category "Skin
Corrosion --- Category 1" or the category "Skin Irritation --- Category
2".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[In vitro]{.otherLang lang="la"} or [ex
vivo]{.otherLang lang="la"} data

**[[8.2.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.2.4 .sectionLabel}** A substance for
which the data, [in vitro]{.otherLang lang="la"} or [ex vivo]{.otherLang
lang="la"}, acquired from a scientifically validated method for the
evaluation of skin corrosion or skin irritation demonstrate that the
substance is skin-corrosive or skin-irritant is classified,
respectively, in the category "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1" or the
category "Skin Irritation --- Category 2".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}pH

**[[8.2.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.2.5 .sectionLabel}** A substance for
which the pH is less than or equal to two or equal to or greater than
11.5 is classified in the category "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1",
unless an assessment of alkali or acid reserve performed in accordance
with established scientific principles supports the conclusion that it
need not be classified as a skin-corrosive substance on the basis of its
pH.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Structure-activity relationship --- skin
corrosion

- **[[8.2.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.2.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A substance for which a
  structure-activity relationship, established in accordance with
  established scientific principles, supports the conclusion that the
  substance must be classified in the category "Skin Corrosion ---
  Category 1" is classified in that category.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Structure-activity relationship ---
  skin irritation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A substance for which a structure-activity
  relationship, established in accordance with established scientific
  principles, supports the conclusion that the substance must be
  classified in the category "Skin Irritation --- Category 2" is
  classified in that category.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Totality of available data

**[[8.2.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.2.7 .sectionLabel}** A substance for
which an evaluation of the totality of available data, performed in
accordance with established scientific principles, supports the
conclusion that the substance is skin-corrosive or skin-irritant is
classified, respectively, in the category "Skin Corrosion --- Category
1" or the category "Skin Irritation --- Category 2".

##### [Classification of Mixtures]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819584 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of provisions

**[[8.2.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.2.8 .sectionLabel}** The classification
of a mixture as skin-corrosive or as skin-irritant in a category or
subcategory of this hazard class must proceed in accordance with the
order of sections 8.2.9 to 8.2.11.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for mixture as a whole

- **[[8.2.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.2.9
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If data of the types referred to in
  subparagraphs 2.1(a)(i) to (iv) are available for the mixture as a
  whole, the mixture must be classified in accordance with the order of
  sections 8.2.2 to 8.2.7, unless under subsection 8.2.2(4) the mixture
  need not be classified.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for mixture as a whole
  --- sections 8.2.10 and 8.2.11

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If data of the types referred to in subparagraphs
  2.1(a)(i) to (iv) are available for the mixture as a whole, but the
  mixture cannot be classified further to subsections 8.2.2(1) to (3) or
  sections 8.2.3 to 8.2.7, its classification in a category or
  subcategory of this hazard class must proceed in accordance with the
  order of sections 8.2.10 and 8.2.11.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for use of bridging
principles

**[[8.2.10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.2.10 .sectionLabel}** If data are
available to enable the characterization of the mixture as a
skin-corrosive mixture or a skin-irritant mixture, in accordance with
the bridging principles referred to in subsections 2.3(3) to (8), the
mixture must be classified in a category or subcategory of this hazard
class in accordance with those subsections.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for ingredients

- **[[8.2.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.2.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), a mixture
  that contains one or more ingredients that are classified in the
  category "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1", the subcategory "Skin
  Corrosion --- Category 1A", the subcategory "Skin Corrosion ---
  Category 1B", the subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1C" or the
  category "Skin Irritation --- Category 2" is classified in a category
  or subcategory of this hazard class in accordance with subsection (2),
  subject to the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} ingredients that are classified in the category
    "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1", the subcategory "Skin Corrosion ---
    Category 1A", the subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1B", the
    subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1C" or the category "Skin
    Irritation --- Category 2" and are present in the mixture at a
    concentration equal to or greater than the concentration limit of
    1.0% must be included in the calculation of the sum of
    concentrations of ingredients; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ingredients that are classified in the category
    "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1", the subcategory "Skin Corrosion ---
    Category 1A", the subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1B", the
    subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1C" or the category "Skin
    Irritation --- Category 2" and are present in the mixture at a
    concentration of less than the concentration limit of 1.0% must be
    included in the calculation of the sum of concentrations of
    ingredients only if there is evidence that, at the concentration at
    which they are present, the ingredients are skin-corrosive
    substances or skin-irritant substances.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Classification --- mixture

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A mixture is classified in a category of this hazard
  class in accordance with the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the sum of concentrations of ingredients
    classified in the category "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1" is equal
    to or greater than 5.0%, the mixture is classified in the category
    "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1";

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} if the sum of concentrations of ingredients
    classified in the subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1A" is
    equal to or greater than 5.0%, and the mixture does not contain any
    ingredients classified in the category "Skin Corrosion --- Category
    1", the subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1B", or the
    subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1C", the mixture is
    classified in the category "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1" or the
    subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1A";

  - [(a.2)]{.lawlabel} if the sum of concentrations of ingredients
    classified in the subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1B" is
    equal to or greater than 5.0%, and the mixture does not contain any
    ingredients classified in the category "Skin Corrosion --- Category
    1", the subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1A", or the
    subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1C", the mixture is
    classified in the category "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1" or the
    subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1B";

  - [(a.3)]{.lawlabel} if the sum of concentrations of ingredients
    classified in the subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1C" is
    equal to or greater than 5.0%, and the mixture does not contain any
    ingredients classified in the category "Skin Corrosion --- Category
    1", the subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1A", or the
    subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1B", the mixture is
    classified in the category "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1" or the
    subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1C";

  - [(a.4)]{.lawlabel} if the sum of the concentrations of ingredients
    set out in two or more of the following subparagraphs is equal to or
    greater than 5.0%, the mixture is classified in the category "Skin
    Corrosion ─ Category 1":

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} ingredients classified in the category "Skin
      Corrosion ─ Category 1",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} ingredients classified in the subcategory "Skin
      Corrosion ─ Category 1A",

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} ingredients classified in the subcategory "Skin
      Corrosion ─ Category 1B", and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} ingredients classified in the subcategory "Skin
      Corrosion ─ Category 1C";

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the sum of concentrations of ingredients
    classified in the category "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1",
    ingredients classified in the subcategory "Skin Corrosion ---
    Category 1A", ingredients classified in the subcategory "Skin
    Corrosion --- Category 1B" and ingredients classified in the
    subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1C" is equal to or greater
    than 1.0% but less than 5.0%, the mixture is classified in the
    category "Skin Irritation --- Category 2";

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the sum of concentrations of ingredients
    classified in the category "Skin Irritation --- Category 2" is equal
    to or greater than 10.0%, the mixture is classified in the category
    "Skin Irritation --- Category 2"; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the sum of the results of the following
    subparagraphs is equal to or greater than 10.0%, the mixture is
    classified in the category "Skin Irritation --- Category 2":

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 10 times the sum of concentrations of ingredients
      classified in the category "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1",
      ingredients classified in the subcategory "Skin Corrosion ---
      Category 1A", ingredients classified in the subcategory "Skin
      Corrosion --- Category 1B" and ingredients classified in the
      subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1C", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the sum of concentrations of ingredients
      classified in the category "Skin Irritation --- Category 2".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mixtures containing particular
  ingredients

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A mixture is classified in a category of this hazard
  class in accordance with the following table if it contains one or
  more substances, such as acids, bases, inorganic salts, aldehydes,
  phenols or surfactants, which could be corrosive or irritant at
  concentrations below the concentration limits set out in
  subsection (2) and at least one ingredient with a concentration that
  is above the concentration limits set out below:

  <figure id="819609">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize7 topdouble topbot">

### [SUBPART 3]{.HLabel2}[Serious Eye Damage/Eye Irritation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-819615 .Subheading}

#### [Definitions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819616 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[8.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.3 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Subpart.

[[eye irritation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[eye irritation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the production of
    changes in the eye after exposure of the eye to a mixture or
    substance that are fully reversible within an observation period of
    21 days after that exposure. ([irritation oculaire]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[serious eye damage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[serious eye damage]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the production of
    tissue damage in the eye or serious physical decay of vision,
    occurring after exposure of the eye to a mixture or substance,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for which data demonstrate that it is
      irreversible; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that is not fully reversible within an
      observation period of 21 days after that exposure. ([lésion
      oculaire grave]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote

  role="button"}

#### [Classification in a Category or Subcategory of the Class]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819623 .Subheading}

##### [Classification of Substances]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819624 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of provisions

**[[8.3.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.3.1 .sectionLabel}** The classification
of a substance that causes serious eye damage or eye irritation in a
category or subcategory of this hazard class must proceed in accordance
with the order of sections 8.3.2 to 8.3.7, unless, after applying
subsections 8.3.2(1) to (4), the substance is not classified further to
subsection 8.3.2(5).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Human or animal data --- serious eye
damage

- **[[8.3.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.3.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A substance for which there are
  human data or purposely generated animal data with respect to serious
  eye damage is classified in the category of this hazard class in
  accordance with the following table:

  <figure id="819630">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom"></th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Category</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Criteria</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight top">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight top">Serious Eye Damage — Category 1</td>
  <td class="top"><p>A substance</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> that, according to human
  data, causes serious eye damage;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> that, according to animal
  data acquired from a scientifically validated method from at least one
  animal, produces effects on the cornea, iris or conjunctiva</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> that are irreversible as
  demonstrated by data, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> that are not fully
  reversible within an observation period of 21 days; or</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> in respect of which animal
  data acquired from tests performed in accordance with the OECD Guideline
  for the Testing of Chemicals, No. 405, entitled Acute Eye
  Irritation/Corrosion, as amended from time to time, demonstrate a
  positive response in at least two of three animals, and the mean score
  calculated following gradings at 24, 48 and 72 hours after instillation
  of the substance, is</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> in the case of corneal
  opacity, ≥ 3, or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> in the case of iritis, &gt;
  1.5</p></li>
  </ul></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Human data --- eye irritation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A substance for which human data demonstrate that it
  causes eye irritation is classified in the category "Eye Irritation
  --- Category 2".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Animal data --- eye irritation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A substance for which purposely generated animal data
  demonstrate that it causes eye irritation is classified in the
  category "Eye Irritation --- Category 2" and is, if the applicable
  data are available, further classified in the appropriate subcategory
  in accordance with the following table:

  <figure id="819640">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize7 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom"></th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 3</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Category</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Subcategory</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Criteria</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">Eye Irritation —
  Category 2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Eye Irritation — Category
  2A</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom top"><p>A substance that is not classified in
  the category “Serious Eye Damage — Category 1” and in respect of which
  animal data acquired from tests performed in accordance with the OECD
  Guideline for the Testing of Chemicals, No. 405, entitled Acute Eye
  Irritation/Corrosion, as amended from time to time, demonstrate in at
  least two of three animals a positive response that fully reverses
  within an observation period of more than seven days but not more than
  21 days, and the mean score calculated following gradings at 24, 48 and
  72 hours after instillation of the substance, is</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> in the case of corneal
  opacity, ≥ 1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> in the case of iritis, ≥
  1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> in the case of conjunctival
  redness, ≥ 2; or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> in the case of conjunctival
  edema (chemosis), ≥ 2</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight" scope="row">Eye Irritation — Category 2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight">Eye Irritation — Category 2B</td>
  <td><p>A substance that is not classified in the category “Serious Eye
  Damage — Category 1” and in respect of which animal data acquired from
  tests performed in accordance with the OECD Guideline for the Testing of
  Chemicals, No. 405, entitled Acute Eye Irritation/Corrosion, as amended
  from time to time, demonstrate in at least two of three animals a
  positive response that fully reverses within an observation period of
  seven days, and the mean score calculated following gradings at 24, 48
  and 72 hours after instillation of the substance, is</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> in the case of corneal
  opacity, ≥ 1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> in the case of iritis, ≥
  1;</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> in the case of conjunctival
  redness, ≥ 2; or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> in the case of conjunctival
  edema (chemosis), ≥ 2</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Skin corrosion data

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A substance that is classified in the category "Skin
  Corrosion --- Category 1" in accordance with subsections 8.2.2(1)
  and (2) is also classified in the category "Serious Eye Damage ---
  Category 1" of this hazard class.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No classification

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A substance that meets the following conditions need
  not be classified in any category of this hazard class:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the substance is not classified further to
    subsections (1) to (4); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} human data or purposely generated animal data on
    the substance, acquired from a scientifically validated method, with
    respect to serious eye damage or eye irritation, demonstrate that
    the substance does not cause serious eye damage or eye irritation.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other animal data --- eye or skin
exposure

**[[8.3.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.3.3 .sectionLabel}** A substance for
which there are animal data on eye exposure that demonstrate, or animal
data on skin exposure that support the conclusion, that the substance
causes serious eye damage or eye irritation is classified in the
category "Serious Eye Damage --- Category 1" or the category "Eye
Irritation --- Category 2", and, in the latter case, if the applicable
data are available, the substance is further classified in the
subcategory "Eye Irritation --- Category 2A" or in the subcategory "Eye
Irritation --- Category 2B".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[In vitro]{.otherLang lang="la"} or [ex
vivo]{.otherLang lang="la"} data --- serious eye damage

- **[[8.3.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.3.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A substance for which the data, [in
  vitro]{.otherLang lang="la"} or [ex vivo]{.otherLang lang="la"},
  acquired from a scientifically validated method for the evaluation of
  serious eye damage demonstrate that the substance causes serious eye
  damage is classified in the category "Serious Eye Damage --- Category
  1".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[In vitro]{.otherLang lang="la"} or [ex
  vivo]{.otherLang lang="la"} data --- eye irritation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A substance for which the data, [in vitro]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} or [ex vivo]{.otherLang lang="la"}, acquired from a
  scientifically validated method for the evaluation of eye irritation
  demonstrate that the substance causes eye irritation is classified in
  the category "Eye Irritation --- Category 2" and, if the applicable
  data are available, the substance is further classified in the
  subcategory "Eye Irritation --- Category 2A" or in the subcategory
  "Eye Irritation --- Category 2B".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}pH

**[[8.3.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.3.5 .sectionLabel}** A substance for
which the pH is less than or equal to two or equal to or greater than
11.5 is classified in the category "Serious Eye Damage --- Category 1",
unless an assessment of alkali or acid reserve performed in accordance
with established scientific principles supports the conclusion that it
need not be classified as a substance that causes serious eye damage on
the basis of its pH.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Structure-activity relationship ---
serious eye damage

- **[[8.3.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.3.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A substance for which a
  structure-activity relationship, established in accordance with
  established scientific principles, supports the conclusion that the
  substance must be classified in the category "Serious Eye Damage ---
  Category 1" is classified in that category.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Structure-activity relationship --- eye
  irritation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A substance for which a structure-activity
  relationship, established in accordance with established scientific
  principles, supports the conclusion that the substance must be
  classified in the category "Eye Irritation --- Category 2" is
  classified in that category and, if the applicable data are available,
  the substance is further classified in the subcategory "Eye Irritation
  --- Category 2A" or in the subcategory "Eye Irritation --- Category
  2B".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Totality of available data

**[[8.3.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.3.7 .sectionLabel}** A substance for
which an evaluation of the totality of available data, performed in
accordance with established scientific principles, supports the
conclusion that the substance causes serious eye damage or eye
irritation is classified in the category "Serious Eye Damage ---
Category 1" or the category "Eye Irritation --- Category 2" and, in the
latter case, if the applicable data are available, the substance is
further classified in the subcategory "Eye Irritation --- Category 2A"
or in the subcategory "Eye Irritation --- Category 2B".

##### [Classification of Mixtures]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819668 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of provisions

**[[8.3.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.3.8 .sectionLabel}** The classification
of a mixture as a mixture that causes serious eye damage or eye
irritation in a category of this hazard class must proceed in accordance
with the order of sections 8.3.9 to 8.3.11.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for mixture as a whole

- **[[8.3.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.3.9
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If data of the types referred to in
  subparagraphs 2.1(a)(i) to (iv) are available for the mixture as a
  whole, the mixture must be classified in accordance with the order of
  sections 8.3.2 to 8.3.7 unless, under subsection 8.3.2(5), the mixture
  need not be classified.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for mixture as a whole
  --- sections 8.3.10 and 8.3.11

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If data of the types referred to in subparagraphs
  2.1(a)(i) to (iv) are available for the mixture as a whole, but the
  mixture cannot be classified further to subsections 8.3.2(1) to (4) or
  sections 8.3.3 to 8.3.7, its classification in a category or
  subcategory of this hazard class must proceed in accordance with the
  order of sections 8.3.10 and 8.3.11.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for use of bridging
principles

**[[8.3.10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.3.10 .sectionLabel}** If data are
available to enable the characterization of the mixture as a mixture
that causes serious eye damage or eye irritation, in accordance with the
bridging principles referred to in subsections 2.3(3) to (8), the
mixture must be classified in a category or subcategory of this hazard
class in accordance with those subsections.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for ingredients

- **[[8.3.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.3.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), a mixture
  that contains one or more ingredients that are classified in the
  category "Serious Eye Damage --- Category 1", the category "Eye
  Irritation --- Category 2", the subcategory "Eye Irritation ---
  Category 2A" or the subcategory "Eye Irritation --- Category 2B" is
  classified in a category or subcategory of this hazard class in
  accordance with subsection (2), subject to the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} ingredients that are classified in the category
    "Serious Eye Damage --- Category 1", the category "Eye Irritation
    --- Category 2", the subcategory "Eye Irritation --- Category 2A" or
    the subcategory "Eye Irritation --- Category 2B" and are present in
    the mixture at a concentration equal to or greater than the
    concentration limit of 1.0% must be included in the calculation of
    the sum of concentrations of ingredients; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ingredients that are classified in the category
    "Serious Eye Damage --- Category 1", the category "Eye Irritation
    --- Category 2", the subcategory "Eye Irritation --- Category 2A" or
    the subcategory "Eye Irritation --- Category 2B" and are present in
    the mixture at a concentration of less than the concentration limit
    of 1.0% must be included in the calculation of the sum of
    concentrations of ingredients only if there is evidence that, at the
    concentration at which they are present, the ingredients are
    substances that cause serious eye damage or eye irritation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Classification --- mixture

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A mixture is classified in a category of this hazard
  class in accordance with the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the sum of concentrations of ingredients
    classified in the category "Serious Eye Damage --- Category 1", the
    category "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1", the subcategory "Skin
    Corrosion -- Category 1A", the subcategory "Skin Corrosion --
    Category 1B" and the subcategory "Skin Corrosion -- Category 1C" is
    equal to or greater than 3.0%, the mixture is classified in the
    category "Serious Eye Damage --- Category 1";

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the sum of concentrations of ingredients
    classified in the category "Serious Eye Damage --- Category 1", the
    category "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1", the subcategory "Skin
    Corrosion -- Category 1A", the subcategory "Skin Corrosion --
    Category 1B" and the subcategory "Skin Corrosion -- Category 1C" is
    equal to or greater than 1.0% but less than 3.0%, the mixture is
    classified in the category "Eye Irritation --- Category 2";

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the sum of concentrations of ingredients
    classified in the category "Eye Irritation --- Category 2" is equal
    to or greater than 10.0%, the mixture is classified in the category
    "Eye Irritation --- Category 2";

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} if the sum of concentrations of ingredients
    classified in the subcategory "Eye Irritation --- Category 2A" is
    equal to or greater than 10.0%, and the mixture does not contain any
    ingredients classified in the category "Eye Irritation --- Category
    2" or the subcategory "Eye Irritation --- Category 2B", the mixture
    is classified in the category "Eye Irritation --- Category 2" or the
    subcategory "Eye Irritation --- Category 2A";

  - [(c.2)]{.lawlabel} if the sum of concentrations of ingredients
    classified in the subcategory "Eye Irritation --- Category 2B" is
    equal to or greater than 10.0% and the mixture does not contain any
    ingredients classified in the category "Eye Irritation --- Category
    2" or the subcategory "Eye Irritation --- Category 2A", the mixture
    is classified in the category "Eye Irritation --- Category 2" or the
    subcategory "Eye Irritation --- Category 2B";

  - [(c.3)]{.lawlabel} if the sum of the concentrations of ingredients
    set out in two or more of the following subparagraphs is equal to or
    greater than 10.0%, the mixture is classified in the category "Eye
    Irritation --- Category 2":

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} ingredients classified in the category "Eye
      Irritation --- Category 2",

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} ingredients classified in the subcategory "Eye
      Irritation --- Category 2A", and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} ingredients classified in the subcategory "Eye
      Irritation --- Category 2B"; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the sum of the results of the following
    subparagraphs is equal to or greater than 10.0%, the mixture is
    classified in the category "Eye Irritation --- Category 2":

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} 10 times the total of the sum of concentrations
      of ingredients classified in the category "Serious Eye Damage ---
      Category 1" and the sum of concentrations of ingredients
      classified in the category "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1",
      ingredients classified in the subcategory "Skin Corrosion ---
      Category 1A", ingredients classified in the subcategory "Skin
      Corrosion --- Category 1B" and ingredients classified in the
      subcategory "Skin Corrosion --- Category 1C", and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the sum of concentrations of ingredients
      classified in the category "Eye Irritation --- Category 2",
      ingredients classified in the subcategory "Eye Irritation ---
      Category 2A" and ingredients classified in the subcategory "Eye
      Irritation --- Category 2B".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mixtures containing particular
  ingredients

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A mixture is classified in a category of this hazard
  class in accordance with the following table if the mixture contains
  one or more substances, such as acids, bases, inorganic salts,
  aldehydes, phenols or surfactants, which could cause serious eye
  damage or eye irritation at concentrations below the concentration
  limits set out in subsection (2) and at least one ingredient with a
  concentration that is above the concentration limits set out below:

### [SUBPART 4]{.HLabel2}[Respiratory or Skin Sensitization]{.HTitleText2} {#h-819699 .Subheading}

#### [Definitions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819700 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[8.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.4 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Subpart.

[[respiratory sensitization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[respiratory sensitization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    production of hypersensitivity of the airways occurring after
    inhalation of a mixture or substance. ([sensibilisation
    respiratoire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[respiratory sensitizer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[respiratory sensitizer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a mixture or
    substance that is liable to lead to hypersensitivity of the airways
    following inhalation. ([sensibilisant respiratoire]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[skin sensitization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[skin sensitization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the production of
    an allergic response occurring after skin contact with a mixture or
    substance. ([sensibilisation cutanée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[skin sensitizer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[skin sensitizer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a mixture or substance
    that is liable to lead to an allergic response following skin
    contact. ([sensibilisant cutané]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote

#### [Classification in a Category or Subcategory of the Class]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819707 .Subheading}

##### [Classification of Substances]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819708 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Respiratory sensitizer --- category

- **[[8.4.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.4.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A substance that is a respiratory
  sensitizer is classified in the category of this hazard class in
  accordance with the following table:

  <figure id="819712">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">

  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom"></th>

  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Category</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Criteria</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight top">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight top">Respiratory Sensitizer — Category 1</td>
  <td class="top"><p>A substance</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> that, according to human
  data, leads to specific respiratory hypersensitivity; or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> in respect of which animal
  data acquired from scientifically validated methods for the evaluation
  of respiratory sensitization demonstrate positive results</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Respiratory sensitizer ---
  subcategories

  Sensitizer --- Category 1" under subsection (1) is, if the applicable
  data are available, further classified in the subcategory "Respiratory
  Sensitizer --- Category 1A" or in the subcategory "Respiratory
  Sensitizer --- Category 1B" in accordance with the following table:

  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 3</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Category</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Subcategory</th>

  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">Respiratory
  Sensitizer — Category 1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Respiratory Sensitizer —

  <td class="noBorderBottom top"><p>A substance</p>

  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> that, according to human

  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> in respect of which animal

  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> in respect of which animal

##### [Classification of Mixtures]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819742 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of provisions

**[[8.4.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.4.2 .sectionLabel}** The classification
of a mixture as a respiratory sensitizer or a skin sensitizer, or both,
in one or more categories of this hazard class must proceed in
accordance with the order of sections 8.4.3 to 8.4.5.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for mixture as a whole

**[[8.4.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.4.3 .sectionLabel}** If data of the
types referred to in subparagraphs 2.1(a)(i) to (iv) are available for
the mixture as a whole, the mixture must be classified as a respiratory
sensitizer or a skin sensitizer, or both, in accordance with section
8.4.1.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for use of bridging
principles

**[[8.4.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.4.4 .sectionLabel}** If data are
available to enable the characterization of the mixture as a respiratory
sensitizer or a skin sensitizer, or both, in accordance with the
bridging principles referred to in subsections 2.3(3) to (8), the
mixture must be classified in a category of this hazard class in
accordance with those subsections.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for ingredients

**[[8.4.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.4.5 .sectionLabel}** A mixture is
classified as a respiratory sensitizer or as a skin sensitizer, or both,
as the case may be, in accordance with the following:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} as a respiratory sensitizer,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the category "Respiratory Sensitizer ---
    Category 1", if it contains at least one ingredient at a
    concentration equal to or greater than the concentration limit of
    0.1% that is classified in the category "Respiratory Sensitizer ---
    Category 1",

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the subcategory "Respiratory Sensitizer ---
    Category 1A", if it contains at least one ingredient at a
    concentration equal to or greater than the concentration limit of
    0.1% that is classified in the subcategory "Respiratory Sensitizer
    --- Category 1A", or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the subcategory "Respiratory Sensitizer ---
    Category 1B", if it does not contain ingredients classified in the
    subcategory "Respiratory Sensitizer --- Category 1A" at a
    concentration equal to or greater than the concentration limit of
    0.1% and

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} it contains at least one ingredient that is a
      solid or a liquid at a concentration equal to or greater than the
      concentration limit of 1.0% that is classified in the subcategory
      "Respiratory Sensitizer --- Category 1B", or

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} it contains at least one ingredient that is a gas
      at a concentration equal to or greater than the concentration
      limit of 0.2% that is classified in the subcategory "Respiratory
      Sensitizer --- Category 1B"; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} as a skin sensitizer,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the category "Skin Sensitizer --- Category 1",
    if it contains at least one ingredient at a concentration equal to
    or greater than the concentration limit of 0.1% that is classified
    in the category "Skin Sensitizer --- Category 1",

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the subcategory "Skin Sensitizer --- Category
    1A", if it contains at least one ingredient at a concentration equal
    to or greater than the concentration limit of 0.1% that is
    classified in the subcategory "Skin Sensitizer --- Category 1A", or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the subcategory "Skin Sensitizer --- Category
    1B", if it does not contain ingredients classified in the
    subcategory "Skin Sensitizer --- Category 1A" at a concentration
    equal to or greater than the concentration limit of 0.1% and it
    contains at least one ingredient at a concentration equal to or
    greater than the concentration limit of 1.0% that is classified in
    the subcategory "Skin Sensitizer --- Category 1B".

### [SUBPART 5]{.HLabel2}[Germ Cell Mutagenicity]{.HTitleText2} {#h-819761 .Subheading}

#### [Definitions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819762 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[8.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.5 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Subpart.

[[genotoxicity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[genotoxicity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the alteration of the
    structure, information content or segregation of DNA by an agent or
    process, including those agents or processes that cause DNA damage
    by interfering with normal replication processes or that in a
    non-physiological manner temporarily alter its
    replication. ([génotoxicité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[germ cell mutagen]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[germ cell mutagen]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a mixture or
    substance that is liable to lead to an increased occurrence of
    mutations in the germ cells of a population. ([mutagène des cellules
    germinales]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[germ cell mutagenicity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[germ cell mutagenicity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an increased
    occurrence of heritable gene mutations, including heritable
    structural and numerical chromosome aberrations in germ cells,
    occurring after exposure to a mixture or substance. ([mutagénicité
    sur les cellules germinales]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[mutagenic]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[mutagenic]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in relation to a mixture or
    substance, liable to lead to an increased occurrence of mutations in
    populations of cells or organisms. ([mutagène]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[mutagenicity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[mutagenicity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an increased occurrence
    of mutations in populations of cells or
    organisms. ([mutagénicité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[mutation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[mutation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a permanent change in the
    amount or structure of the genetic material in a cell and includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the heritable genetic changes that may be
      manifested at the phenotypic level; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the underlying DNA modifications when known,
      including specific base pair changes and chromosomal
      translocations. ([mutation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote

  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1386291-1387272.html"
  role="button"}

#### [Classification in a Category or Subcategory of the Class]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819772 .Subheading}

##### [Classification of Substances]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819773 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Categories

**[[8.5.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.5.1 .sectionLabel}** A substance that is
a germ cell mutagen is classified in a category or subcategory of this
hazard class in accordance with the following table:

<figure id="819776">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize7 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom"></th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 3</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Category</th>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Subcategory</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Criteria</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">Germ Cell
Mutagenicity — Category 1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Germ Cell Mutagenicity —
Category 1A</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">A substance that, according to data from
human epidemiological studies, induces heritable mutations in germ
cells</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">Germ Cell
Mutagenicity — Category 1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Germ Cell Mutagenicity —
Category 1B</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>A substance in respect of which</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> data acquired from <span
class="otherLang" lang="la">in vivo</span> heritable germ cell
mutagenicity tests in mammals demonstrate positive results;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> data acquired from <span
class="otherLang" lang="la">in vivo</span> somatic cell mutagenicity
tests in mammals demonstrate positive results and there is evidence that
the substance has the potential to cause mutations to germ cells, such
as</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> in germ cells, positive <span
class="otherLang" lang="la">in vivo</span> mutagenicity test results or
positive <span class="otherLang" lang="la">in vivo</span> genotoxicity
test results, or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> evidence that the substance
or any of its metabolites is able to interact with the genetic material
of germ cells; or</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> data on human germ cells
demonstrate mutagenic effects, with or without demonstrating
transmission to offspring, including an increase in the frequency of
aneuploidy in sperm of men exposed to the substance</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight" scope="row">Germ Cell Mutagenicity — Category
2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight"></td>
<td><p>A substance in respect of which</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> data acquired from <span
class="otherLang" lang="la">in vivo</span> somatic cell mutagenicity
tests in mammals demonstrate positive results;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> data acquired from <span
class="otherLang" lang="la">in vivo</span> somatic cell genotoxicity
tests demonstrate positive results and data acquired from <span
class="otherLang" lang="la">in vitro</span> mutagenicity tests
demonstrate positive results; or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> data acquired from <span
class="otherLang" lang="la">in vitro</span> mutagenicity tests in
mammalian cells demonstrate positive results and the substance has a
structure-activity relationship with germ cell mutagens classified in
the subcategory “Germ Cell Mutagenicity — Category 1A”</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

##### [Classification of Mixtures]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819782 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of provisions

**[[8.5.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.5.2 .sectionLabel}** The classification
of a mixture as a germ cell mutagen in a category or subcategory of this
hazard class must proceed in accordance with the order of sections 8.5.3
to 8.5.5.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ingredient classified in Category 1 or 1A

- **[[8.5.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.5.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A mixture is classified in the
  category "Germ Cell Mutagenicity --- Category 1" or the subcategory
  "Germ Cell Mutagenicity --- Category 1A" if it contains at least one
  ingredient at a concentration equal to or greater than the
  concentration limit of 0.1% that is classified in the subcategory
  "Germ Cell Mutagenicity --- Category 1A", unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are data for the mixture as a whole that
    demonstrate conclusively, based on established scientific
    principles, that the mixture is a germ cell mutagen, in which case
    the mixture is classified as a germ cell mutagen in accordance with
    section 8.5.1; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the mixture as a whole has been subjected to an [in
    vivo]{.otherLang lang="la"} heritable germ cell mutagenicity test
    that determines that the mixture is not a germ cell mutagen, and a
    scientifically validated method was used and the test was performed
    in accordance with generally accepted standards of good scientific
    practice at the time it was carried out.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ingredient classified in Category 1 or
  1B

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A mixture is classified in the category "Germ Cell
  Mutagenicity --- Category 1" or the subcategory "Germ Cell
  Mutagenicity --- Category 1B" if it contains at least one ingredient
  at a concentration equal to or greater than the concentration limit of
  0.1% that is classified in the subcategory "Germ Cell Mutagenicity ---
  Category 1B", and does not contain any ingredient at a concentration
  equal to or greater than the concentration limit of 0.1% that is
  classified in the subcategory "Germ Cell Mutagenicity --- Category
  1A", unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are data for the mixture as a whole that
    demonstrate conclusively, based on established scientific
    principles, that the mixture is a germ cell mutagen, in which case
    the mixture is classified as a germ cell mutagen in accordance with
    section 8.5.1; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the mixture as a whole has been subjected to an [in
    vivo]{.otherLang lang="la"} heritable germ cell mutagenicity test
    that determines that the mixture is not a germ cell mutagen, and a
    scientifically validated method was used and the test was performed
    in accordance with generally accepted standards of good scientific
    practice at the time it was carried out.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-272, s. 48](#1386293-1387283){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1386293-1387283.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-8.5.3-20150211.html "Link to previous version of section 8.5.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ingredient classified in Category 2

**[[8.5.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.5.4 .sectionLabel}** A mixture is
classified in the category "Germ Cell Mutagenicity --- Category 2" if it
contains at least one ingredient at a concentration equal to or greater
than the concentration limit of 1.0% that is classified in the category
"Germ Cell Mutagenicity --- Category 2", unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are data for the mixture as a whole that
  demonstrate conclusively, based on established scientific principles,
  that the mixture is a germ cell mutagen, in which case the mixture is
  classified as a germ cell mutagen in accordance with section 8.5.1; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the mixture as a whole has been subjected to an [in
  vivo]{.otherLang lang="la"} heritable germ cell mutagenicity test that
  determines that the mixture is not a germ cell mutagen, and a
  scientifically validated method was used and the test was performed in
  accordance with generally accepted standards of good scientific
  practice at the time it was carried out.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for use of bridging
principles

**[[8.5.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.5.5 .sectionLabel}** If data are
available to enable the characterization of the mixture as a germ cell
mutagen, in accordance with the bridging principles referred to in
subsections 2.3(3), (4) and (7), the mixture must be classified in
accordance with those subsections.

### [SUBPART 6]{.HLabel2}[Carcinogenicity]{.HTitleText2} {#h-819795 .Subheading}

#### [Definitions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819796 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2022-272, s. 49

\]
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[8.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.6 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Subpart.

[[carcinogenic]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[carcinogenic]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in relation to a mixture
    or substance, liable to lead to cancer or to increase the incidence
    of cancer. ([cancérogène]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[carcinogenicity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[carcinogenicity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the production of
    cancer or an increase in the incidence of cancer occurring after
    exposure to a mixture or
    substance. ([cancérogénicité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-272, s. 50](#1386297-1387292){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1386297-1387292.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-8.6-20150211.html "Link to previous version of section 8.6"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Classification in a Category or Subcategory of the Class]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819799 .Subheading}

##### [Classification of Substances]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819800 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Categories

**[[8.6.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.6.1 .sectionLabel}** A carcinogenic
substance is classified in a category or subcategory of this hazard
class in accordance with the following table:

<figure id="819803">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize7 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom"></th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 3</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Category</th>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
scope="col">Subcategory</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Criteria</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">Carcinogenicity —
Category 1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Carcinogenicity — Category
1A</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top">A substance in respect of which human
data establish a causal relationship between exposure to the substance
and the development of cancer</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">Carcinogenicity —
Category 1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Carcinogenicity — Category
1B</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>A substance in respect of which</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> human data establish a causal
relationship between exposure to the substance and the development of
cancer, but there are additional data that do not support, based on
established scientific principles, the conclusion that the substance is
the causative agent;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> animal data establish a
causal relationship between exposure to the substance and an increased
incidence of malignant neoplasms or a combination of benign and
malignant neoplasms in</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> two or more species of
animals, as demonstrated by one or more studies,</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> one species of animal, as
demonstrated by two or more independent studies carried out at different
times, in different laboratories or under different protocols,
or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(iii)</span> one species of animal, as
demonstrated by a single study, if the neoplasms observed in the study
are, based on established scientific principles, atypical in relation to
the incidence, site, type or age at onset for the species of animal
under study; or</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> human data support a positive
association between exposure to the substance and the development of
cancer, and animal data support a positive association between exposure
to the substance and an increased incidence of malignant or benign
neoplasms, but the data supporting either positive association do not
support a conclusion of a causal relationship, based on established
scientific principles</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight" scope="row">Carcinogenicity — Category 2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight"></td>
<td><p>A substance in respect of which</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> human data support a positive
association between exposure to the substance and the development of
cancer, but do not support a conclusion of a causal relationship, based
on established scientific principles; or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> animal data support a
positive association between exposure to the substance and an increased
incidence of malignant or benign neoplasms, but do not support a
conclusion of a causal relationship, based on established scientific
principles</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

##### [Classification of Mixtures]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819809 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of provisions

**[[8.6.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.6.2 .sectionLabel}** The classification
of a mixture as a carcinogenic mixture in a category or subcategory of
this hazard class must proceed in accordance with the order of sections
8.6.3 to 8.6.5.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ingredient classified in Category 1 or 1A

- **[[8.6.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.6.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A mixture is classified in the
  category "Carcinogenicity --- Category 1" or the subcategory
  "Carcinogenicity --- Category 1A" if it contains at least one
  ingredient at a concentration equal to or greater than the
  concentration limit of 0.1% that is classified in the subcategory
  "Carcinogenicity --- Category 1A", unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are data for the mixture as a whole that
    demonstrate conclusively, based on established scientific
    principles, that the mixture is carcinogenic, in which case the
    mixture is classified as a carcinogenic mixture in accordance with
    section 8.6.1; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the mixture as a whole has been subjected to a
    carcinogenicity study that determines that the mixture is not
    carcinogenic, and a scientifically validated method was used and the
    study was performed in accordance with generally accepted standards
    of good scientific practice at the time it was carried out.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ingredient classified in Category 1 or
  1B

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A mixture is classified in the category
  "Carcinogenicity --- Category 1" or the subcategory "Carcinogenicity
  --- Category 1B" if it contains at least one ingredient at a
  concentration equal to or greater than the concentration limit of 0.1%
  that is classified in the subcategory "Carcinogenicity --- Category
  1B", and does not contain any ingredient at a concentration equal to
  or greater than the concentration limit of 0.1% that is classified in
  the subcategory "Carcinogenicity --- Category 1A", unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are data for the mixture as a whole that
    demonstrate conclusively, based on established scientific
    principles, that the mixture is carcinogenic, in which case the
    mixture is classified as a carcinogenic mixture in accordance with
    section 8.6.1; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the mixture as a whole has been subjected to a
    carcinogenicity study that determines that the mixture is not
    carcinogenic, and a scientifically validated method was used and the
    study was performed in accordance with generally accepted standards
    of good scientific practice at the time it was carried out.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-272, s. 51](#1386299-1387303){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1386299-1387303.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-8.6.3-20150211.html "Link to previous version of section 8.6.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ingredient classified in Category 2

**[[8.6.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.6.4 .sectionLabel}** A mixture is
classified in the category "Carcinogenicity --- Category 2" if it
contains at least one ingredient at a concentration equal to or greater
than the concentration limit of 0.1% that is classified in the category
"Carcinogenicity --- Category 2", unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are data for the mixture as a whole that
  demonstrate conclusively, based on established scientific principles,
  that the mixture is carcinogenic, in which case the mixture is
  classified as a carcinogenic mixture in accordance with section 8.6.1;
  or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the mixture as a whole has been subjected to a
  carcinogenicity study that determines that the mixture is not
  carcinogenic, and a scientifically validated method was used and the
  study was performed in accordance with generally accepted standards of
  good scientific practice at the time it was carried out.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for use of bridging
principles

**[[8.6.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.6.5 .sectionLabel}** If data are
available to enable the characterization of the mixture as carcinogenic,
in accordance with the bridging principles referred to in subsections
2.3(3), (4) and (7), the mixture must be classified in accordance with
those subsections.

### [SUBPART 7]{.HLabel2}[Reproductive Toxicity]{.HTitleText2} {#h-819822 .Subheading}

#### [Definitions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819823 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[8.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.7 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Subpart.

[[adverse effects on sexual function and fertility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[adverse effects on sexual function and
    fertility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any effect of a mixture or
    substance that is liable to interfere with sexual function or
    fertility, including

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} alterations to the female or male reproductive
      system;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} adverse effects on onset of puberty, gamete
      production or transport, the reproductive cycle, sexual behaviour,
      parturition or pregnancy outcomes;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} premature reproductive senescence; or

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any modifications to other functions that are
      dependent on the integrity of the reproductive system. ([effets
      néfastes sur la fonction sexuelle et la
      fertilité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[adverse effects on the development of the embryo, fetus or offspring]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[adverse effects on the development of the embryo, fetus or
    offspring]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any adverse effects of a
    mixture or substance on the embryo, fetus or offspring, resulting
    from exposure of either parent to the mixture or substance prior to
    conception or exposure of the developing embryo or fetus to the
    mixture or substance during prenatal development, or of the
    offspring during postnatal development to the time of sexual
    maturation, that is manifested at any point in the development of
    the embryo or fetus, or that is manifested at any point in the
    lifespan of the offspring, and that includes the loss of the embryo
    or fetus, death of the developing offspring, structural abnormality,
    altered growth and functional deficiency. This definition excludes
    the induction of genetically based inheritable effects in the
    offspring. ([effets néfastes sur le développement de l'embryon, du
    foetus ou de la progéniture]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[effects on or via lactation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[effects on or via lactation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any effect of a mixture or substance that
      interferes with lactation; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the presence of the mixture or substance, or its
      metabolites, in the maternal milk in amounts for which there is
      evidence that supports the conclusion, based on established
      scientific principles, that the health of the breast-fed child or
      suckling animal is liable to be threatened. ([effets sur ou via
      l'allaitement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[reproductive toxicity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[reproductive toxicity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} refers to any of the
    following effects occurring after exposure to a mixture or
    substance:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} adverse effects on sexual function and fertility;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} adverse effects on the development of the embryo,
      fetus or offspring; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} effects on or via lactation. ([toxicité pour la
      reproduction]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[toxic to reproduction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[toxic to reproduction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in relation to
    a mixture or substance, liable to lead to reproductive
    toxicity. ([toxique pour la reproduction]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

#### [Classification in a Category or Subcategory of the Class]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819840 .Subheading}

##### [Classification of Substances]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819841 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Categories or subcategories ---
Categories 1A, 1B and 2

- **[[8.7.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.7.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A substance that is toxic to
  reproduction is classified in a category or subcategory of this hazard
  class in accordance with the following table:

  <figure id="819845">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize7 topdouble topbot">
  <thead>
  <tr class="topdouble">
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom"></th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
  <th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 3</th>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Category</th>
  <th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom"
  scope="col">Subcategory</th>
  <th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Criteria</th>
  </tr>
  </thead>
  <tbody>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">Reproductive
  Toxicity — Category 1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Reproductive Toxicity —
  Category 1A</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom top">A substance in respect of which human
  data demonstrate that exposure to the substance leads to adverse effects
  on sexual function and fertility or adverse effects on the development
  of the embryo, fetus or offspring</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">Reproductive
  Toxicity — Category 1</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Reproductive Toxicity —
  Category 1B</td>
  <td class="noBorderBottom"><p>A substance in respect of which animal
  data demonstrate that exposure of the animal to the substance leads to
  the following:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> adverse effects on sexual
  function and fertility or adverse effects on the development of the
  embryo, fetus or offspring, in the absence of other toxic effects;
  or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> adverse effects on sexual
  function and fertility or adverse effects on the development of the
  embryo, fetus or offspring, in the presence of other toxic effects,
  provided that such adverse effects are not considered to be a secondary
  non-specific consequence of the other toxic effects</p></li>
  </ul></td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td class="noBorderRight">3</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight" scope="row">Reproductive Toxicity — Category
  2</td>
  <td class="noBorderRight"></td>
  <td>A substance in respect of which human or animal data support a
  positive association between exposure to the substance and adverse
  effects on sexual function and fertility or adverse effects on the
  development of the embryo, fetus or offspring, but do not support a
  conclusion, based on established scientific principles, that exposure to
  the substance leads to such effects, provided that such effects are not
  considered to be a secondary non-specific consequence of other toxic
  effects</td>
  </tr>
  </tbody>
  </table>
  <figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
  </figure>

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Category --- effects on or via
  lactation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A substance that is toxic to reproduction is
  classified in the category of this hazard class in accordance with the

##### [Classification of Mixtures]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819859 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of provisions

**[[8.7.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.7.2 .sectionLabel}** Subject to
subsection 8.7.5(2), the classification of a mixture as a mixture that
is toxic to reproduction in a category or subcategory of this hazard
class must proceed in accordance with the order of sections 8.7.3 to
8.7.6.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ingredient classified in Category 1 or 1A

- **[[8.7.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.7.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A mixture is classified in the
  category "Reproductive Toxicity --- Category 1" or the subcategory
  "Reproductive Toxicity --- Category 1A" if it contains at least one
  ingredient at a concentration equal to or greater than the
  concentration limit of 0.1% that is classified in the subcategory
  "Reproductive Toxicity --- Category 1A", unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are data for the mixture as a whole that
    demonstrate conclusively, based on established scientific
    principles, that the mixture has adverse effects on sexual function
    and fertility or adverse effects on the development of the embryo,
    fetus or offspring, in which case the mixture is classified as a
    mixture that is toxic to reproduction in accordance with subsection
    8.7.1(1); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the mixture as a whole has been subjected to a
    reproductive toxicity study that determines that the mixture does
    not have adverse effects on sexual function and fertility or adverse
    effects on the development of the embryo, fetus or offspring, and a
    scientifically validated method was used and the study was performed
    in accordance with generally accepted standards of good scientific
    practice at the time it was carried out.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ingredient classified in Category 1 or
  1B

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A mixture is classified in the category "Reproductive
  Toxicity --- Category 1" or the subcategory "Reproductive Toxicity ---
  Category 1B" if it contains at least one ingredient at a concentration
  equal to or greater than the concentration limit of 0.1% that is
  classified in the subcategory "Reproductive Toxicity --- Category 1B",
  and does not contain any ingredient at a concentration equal to or
  greater than the concentration limit of 0.1% that is classified in the
  subcategory "Reproductive Toxicity --- Category 1A", unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are data for the mixture as a whole that
    demonstrate conclusively, based on established scientific
    principles, that the mixture has adverse effects on sexual function
    and fertility or adverse effects on the development of the embryo,
    fetus or offspring, in which case the mixture is classified as a
    mixture that is toxic to reproduction in accordance with subsection
    8.7.1(1); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the mixture as a whole has been subjected to a
    reproductive toxicity study that determines that the mixture does
    not have adverse effects on sexual function and fertility or adverse
    effects on the development of the embryo, fetus or offspring, and a
    scientifically validated method was used and the study was performed
    in accordance with generally accepted standards of good scientific
    practice at the time it was carried out.

::: HistoricalNote

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ingredient classified in Reproductive
Toxicity --- Category 2

**[[8.7.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.7.4 .sectionLabel}** A mixture is
classified in the category "Reproductive Toxicity --- Category 2" if it
contains at least one ingredient at a concentration equal to or greater
than the concentration limit of 0.1% that is classified in the category
"Reproductive Toxicity --- Category 2", unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are data for the mixture as a whole that
  demonstrate conclusively, based on established scientific principles,
  that the mixture has adverse effects on sexual function and fertility
  or adverse effects on the development of the embryo, fetus or
  offspring, in which case the mixture is classified as a mixture that
  is toxic to reproduction in accordance with subsection 8.7.1(1); or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the mixture as a whole has been subjected to a
  reproductive toxicity study that determines that the mixture does not
  have adverse effects on sexual function and fertility or adverse
  effects on the development of the embryo, fetus or offspring, and a
  scientifically validated method was used and the study was performed
  in accordance with generally accepted standards of good scientific
  practice at the time it was carried out.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ingredient classified in Reproductive
Toxicity --- Effects on or via Lactation

- **[[8.7.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.7.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A mixture is classified in the
  category "Reproductive Toxicity --- Effects on or via Lactation" if it
  contains at least one ingredient at a concentration equal to or
  greater than the concentration limit of 0.1% that is classified in the
  category "Reproductive Toxicity --- Effects on or via Lactation",
  unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} there are data for the mixture as a whole that
    demonstrate conclusively, based on established scientific
    principles, that the mixture has effects on or via lactation, in
    which case the mixture is classified as a mixture that is toxic to
    reproduction in accordance with subsection 8.7.1(2); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the mixture as a whole has been subjected to a
    reproductive toxicity study that determines that the mixture does
    not have effects on or via lactation, and a scientifically validated
    method was used and the study was performed in accordance with
    generally accepted standards of good scientific practice at the time
    it was carried out.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Classification in Category 1A, 1B or 2
  and in Reproductive Toxicity --- Effects on or via Lactation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 2.2(3), a mixture that has been
  classified in accordance with section 8.7.3 or 8.7.4 and meets the
  criteria of subsection (1) is also classified in the category
  "Reproductive Toxicity --- Effects on or via Lactation".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for use of bridging
principles

**[[8.7.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.7.6 .sectionLabel}** If data are
available to enable the characterization of the mixture as toxic to
reproduction in accordance with the bridging principles referred to in
subsections 2.3(3), (4) and (7), the mixture must be classified in
accordance with those subsections, in the following categories:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} "Reproductive Toxicity --- Category 1";

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} "Reproductive Toxicity --- Category 2";

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} "Reproductive Toxicity --- Effects on or via
  Lactation";

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} both "Reproductive Toxicity --- Category 1" and
  "Reproductive Toxicity --- Effects on or via Lactation"; or

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} both "Reproductive Toxicity --- Category 2" and
  "Reproductive Toxicity --- Effects on or via Lactation".

### [SUBPART 8]{.HLabel2}[Specific Target Organ Toxicity --- Single Exposure]{.HTitleText2} {#h-819884 .Subheading}

#### [Definitions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819885 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[8.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.8 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Subpart.

[[narcotic effects]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[narcotic effects]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means central nervous
    system depression that

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in humans, may present as drowsiness, narcosis,
      reduced alertness, loss of reflexes, lack of coordination,
      vertigo, severe headache or nausea and may lead to reduced
      judgment, dizziness, irritability, fatigue, impaired memory
      function, deficits in perception or coordination, prolonged
      reaction time or sleepiness; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in animals, may be observed as lethargy, lack of
      coordination righting reflex, narcosis or ataxia. ([effets
      narcotiques]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[organ]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[organ]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any biological
    system. ([organe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[respiratory tract irritation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[respiratory tract irritation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means localized
    redness, edema, pruritis or irritant effects in the respiratory
    tract that impair its function, whether or not accompanied by cough,
    pain, choking, breathing difficulties or other respiratory
    symptoms. ([irritation des voies respiratoires]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[specific target organ toxicity arising from a single exposure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[specific target organ toxicity arising from a single
    exposure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means specific, non-lethal toxic
    effects on target organs that arise from a single exposure to a
    mixture or substance, including all health effects liable to impair
    function of the body or any of its parts, whether reversible or
    irreversible, immediate or delayed, but excludes effects resulting
    from health hazards addressed by Subparts 1 to 7 and 10 of this
    Part. ([toxicité pour certains organes cibles à la suite d'une
    exposition unique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

#### [Classification in a Category of the Class]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819894 .Subheading}

##### [Classification of Substances]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819895 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Two evaluations

- **[[8.8.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.8.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In order to establish the
  classification of a substance that causes specific target organ
  toxicity arising from a single exposure in one or more categories of
  this hazard class, the substance must be evaluated in accordance with
  all the criteria set out in column 2 of the following table, in
  relation to toxic effects on

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the central nervous system and respiratory tract;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} other specific target organs.

  <ul>

  or</p></li>
  <li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> according to animal data,

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Classification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Following the evaluations referred to in subsection
  (1), the substance is classified in one or more categories of this
  hazard class, based on the results of the evaluations of its toxic
  effects as set out in columns 1 and 2 of the following table, in
  accordance with the corresponding category set out in column 3:
