##### [Classification of Mixtures]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819915 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of provisions

**[[8.8.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.8.2 .sectionLabel}** The classification
of a mixture as a mixture that causes specific target organ toxicity
arising from a single exposure in a category of this hazard class must
proceed in accordance with the order of sections 8.8.3 to 8.8.5.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for mixture as a whole

**[[8.8.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.8.3 .sectionLabel}** If data of the
types referred to in subparagraphs 2.1(a)(i) to (iv) are available for
the mixture as a whole, the mixture must be classified as a mixture that
causes specific target organ toxicity arising from a single exposure in
accordance with section 8.8.1.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for use of bridging
principles

**[[8.8.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.8.4 .sectionLabel}** If data are
available to enable the characterization of the mixture as a mixture
that causes specific target organ toxicity arising from a single
exposure, in accordance with the bridging principles referred to in
subsections 2.3(3) to (8), the mixture must be classified in one or more
categories of this hazard class, based on the table to subsection
8.8.1(2), in accordance with those subsections.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for ingredients ---
Category 1, 2 or 3

- **[[8.8.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.8.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A mixture that contains one or more
  ingredients that are classified as a substance that causes specific
  target organ toxicity arising from a single exposure is classified as
  follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the category "Specific Target Organ Toxicity ---
    Single Exposure --- Category 1", if it contains at least one
    ingredient at a concentration equal to or greater than the
    concentration limit of 1.0% that is classified in the category
    "Specific Target Organ Toxicity --- Single Exposure --- Category 1";

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the category "Specific Target Organ Toxicity ---
    Single Exposure --- Category 2", if it contains at least one
    ingredient at a concentration equal to or greater than the
    concentration limit of 1.0% that is classified in the category
    "Specific Target Organ Toxicity --- Single Exposure --- Category 2";

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the category "Specific Target Organ Toxicity ---
    Single Exposure --- Category 3", if it contains at least one
    ingredient that is classified in the category "Specific Target Organ
    Toxicity --- Single Exposure --- Category 3" that is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} at a concentration equal to or greater than the
      concentration at which the effect is elicited, if known, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} at a concentration equal to or greater than the
      concentration limit of 20.0%; or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2022-272, s. 55\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the category "Specific Target Organ Toxicity ---
    Single Exposure --- Category 3", if it contains at least two
    ingredients that are classified in that category which are present
    individually at a concentration of 1.0% or more and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} such ingredients individually cause a transient
      narcotic effect, and the sum of their concentrations is equal to
      or greater than the concentration limit of 20.0%, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} such ingredients individually cause transient
      respiratory tract irritation, and the sum of their concentrations
      is equal to or greater than the concentration limit of 20.0%.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for ingredients ---
  Categories 1 and 3 or 2 and 3

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection 2.2(3), a mixture that has been
  classified in accordance with paragraph (1)(a) or (b) and meets the
  criteria of paragraph (1)(c) is also classified in the category
  "Specific Target Organ Toxicity --- Single Exposure --- Category 3".

  role="button"}
:::

### [SUBPART 9]{.HLabel2}[Specific Target Organ Toxicity --- Repeated Exposure]{.HTitleText2} {#h-819933 .Subheading}

#### [Definitions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819934 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[8.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.9 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Subpart.

[[organ]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[organ]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any biological
    system. ([organe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[specific target organ toxicity arising from repeated exposure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[specific target organ toxicity arising from repeated
    exposure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means specific toxic effects on
    target organs that arise from repeated exposure to a mixture or
    substance, including all health effects liable to impair function of
    the body or any of its parts, whether reversible or irreversible,
    immediate or delayed, but excludes effects resulting from health
    hazards addressed by Subparts 1 to 7 and 10 of this Part. ([toxicité
    pour certains organes cibles à la suite d'expositions
    répétées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

#### [Classification in a Category of the Class]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819939 .Subheading}

##### [Classification of Substances]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819940 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Categories

**[[8.9.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.9.1 .sectionLabel}** A substance that
causes specific target organ toxicity arising from repeated exposure is
classified in a category of this hazard class in accordance with the
following table:

<figure id="819943">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom"></th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Category</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Criteria</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top">Specific Target Organ
Toxicity — Repeated Exposure — Category 1</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom top"><p>A substance that</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> according to human data,
causes specific target organ toxicity arising from repeated exposure;
or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> according to animal data,
causes specific target organ toxicity arising from repeated exposure at
low exposure concentrations, within the concentration value ranges set
out in Table 3.9.1 of the GHS</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Specific Target Organ Toxicity — Repeated
Exposure — Category 2</td>
<td>A substance that, according to animal data, causes specific target
organ toxicity arising from repeated exposure at moderate exposure
concentrations, within the concentration value ranges set out in Table
3.9.2 of the GHS</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

##### [Classification of Mixtures]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819949 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of provisions

**[[8.9.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.9.2 .sectionLabel}** The classification
of a mixture as a mixture that causes specific target organ toxicity
arising from repeated exposure in a category of this hazard class must
proceed in accordance with the order of sections 8.9.3 to 8.9.5.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for mixture as a whole

**[[8.9.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.9.3 .sectionLabel}** If data of the
types referred to in subparagraphs 2.1(a)(i) to (iv) are available for
the mixture as a whole, the mixture must be classified as a mixture that
causes specific target organ toxicity arising from repeated exposure in
accordance with section 8.9.1.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for use of bridging
principles

**[[8.9.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.9.4 .sectionLabel}** If data are
available to enable the characterization of the mixture as a mixture
that causes specific target organ toxicity arising from repeated
exposure, in accordance with the bridging principles referred to in
subsections 2.3(3) to (8), the mixture must be classified in a category
of this hazard class in accordance with those subsections.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for ingredients

**[[8.9.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.9.5 .sectionLabel}** A mixture that
contains one or more ingredients that are classified as a substance that
causes specific target organ toxicity arising from repeated exposure is
classified as follows:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the category "Specific Target Organ Toxicity ---
  Repeated Exposure --- Category 1", if it contains at least one
  ingredient at a concentration equal to or greater than the
  concentration limit of 1.0% that is classified in the category
  "Specific Target Organ Toxicity --- Repeated Exposure --- Category 1";
  or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the category "Specific Target Organ Toxicity ---
  Repeated Exposure --- Category 2", if it contains at least one
  ingredient at a concentration equal to or greater than the
  concentration limit of 1.0% that is classified in the category
  "Specific Target Organ Toxicity --- Repeated Exposure --- Category 2".

### [SUBPART 10]{.HLabel2}[Aspiration Hazard]{.HTitleText2} {#h-819960 .Subheading}

#### [Definitions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819961 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[8.10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.10 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Subpart.

[[aspiration toxicant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[aspiration toxicant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a mixture or
    substance that is liable to cause aspiration toxicity. ([toxique par
    aspiration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[aspiration toxicity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[aspiration toxicity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes severe acute
    effects, such as chemical pneumonia, varying degrees of pulmonary
    injury or death, following the entry of a liquid or solid directly
    through the oral or nasal cavity, or indirectly from vomiting, into
    the trachea and lower respiratory system. ([toxicité par
    aspiration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

#### [Classification in the Category of the Class]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819966 .Subheading}

##### [Classification of Substances]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819967 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Category

**[[8.10.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.10.1 .sectionLabel}** A substance that
is an aspiration toxicant is classified in the category of this hazard
class in accordance with the following table:

<figure id="819970">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom"></th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Category</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Criteria</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight top">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight top">Aspiration Hazard — Category 1</td>
<td class="top"><p>A substance that</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> according to human data,
produces aspiration toxicity if aspirated; or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> in the case of a liquid
hydrocarbon, has a kinematic viscosity ≤ 20.5 mm<sup>2</sup>/s, measured
at 40°C</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

##### [Classification of Mixtures]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819976 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of provisions

**[[8.10.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.10.2 .sectionLabel}** The
classification of a mixture as an aspiration toxicant in the category of
this hazard class must proceed in accordance with the order of sections
8.10.3 to 8.10.5.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for mixture as a whole

**[[8.10.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.10.3 .sectionLabel}** If data of the
types referred to in subparagraphs 2.1(a)(i) to (iv) are available for
the mixture as a whole, the mixture must be classified as an aspiration
toxicant in accordance with section 8.10.1.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for use of bridging
principles

**[[8.10.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.10.4 .sectionLabel}** If data are
available to enable the characterization of the mixture as an aspiration
toxicant, in accordance with the bridging principles referred to in
subsections 2.3(3) to (7), the mixture must be classified in accordance
with those subsections. However, subsection 2.3(3) does not apply if the
concentration of aspiration toxicant in the mixture is less than the
concentration limit of 10.0%.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for ingredients

**[[8.10.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.10.5 .sectionLabel}** A mixture that
contains one or more ingredients that are classified as an aspiration
toxicant is classified in the category "Aspiration Hazard --- Category
1" if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sum of the concentrations of the ingredients that
  are present individually at a concentration of 1.0% or more and that
  are classified in the category "Aspiration Hazard --- Category 1" is
  equal to or greater than the concentration limit of 10.0% and the
  mixture has a kinematic viscosity less than or equal to 20.5 mm^2^/s,
  measured at 40°C; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it separates into two or more distinct layers, in one
  of which the sum of the concentrations of the ingredients that are
  present individually at a concentration of 1.0% or more and that are
  classified in the category "Aspiration Hazard --- Category 1" is equal
  to or greater than the concentration limit of 10.0% and the kinematic
  viscosity of this layer, measured at 40°C, is less than or equal to
  20.5 mm^2^/s.

### [SUBPART 11]{.HLabel2}[Biohazardous Infectious Materials]{.HTitleText2} {#h-819987 .Subheading}

#### [Definition]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819988 .Subheading}

Definition of [[biohazardous infectious material]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[8.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.11 .sectionLabel}** In this Subpart,
[[biohazardous infectious material]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any
microorganism, nucleic acid or protein that causes or is a probable
cause of infection, with or without toxicity, in humans or animals.

#### [Classification in the Category of the Class]{.HTitleText3} {#h-819991 .Subheading}

##### [Classification of Substances]{.HTitleText4} {#h-819992 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Category

**[[8.11.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.11.1 .sectionLabel}** A substance that
is a biohazardous infectious material is classified in the category of
this hazard class in accordance with the following table:

<figure id="819995">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom"></th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Category</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Criteria</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight top">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight top">Biohazardous Infectious Materials —
Category 1</td>
<td class="top"><p>A biohazardous infectious material that</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> falls into <span
class="DefinedTerm"><dfn>Risk Group 2</dfn></span>, <span
class="DefinedTerm"><dfn>Risk Group 3</dfn></span> or <span
class="DefinedTerm"><dfn>Risk Group 4</dfn></span>, as defined in
subsection 3(1) of the <a href="/eng/acts/H-5.67">Human Pathogens and
Toxins Act</a>; or</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> has been shown to be a cause
or probable cause of infection or infection and toxicity in
animals</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

##### [Classification of Mixtures]{.HTitleText4} {#h-820001 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mixture containing more than one
biohazardous infectious material

**[[8.11.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.11.2 .sectionLabel}** A mixture that
contains one or more ingredients that are classified as a biohazardous
infectious material must be classified in accordance with section
8.11.1.

### [SUBPART 12]{.HLabel2}[Health Hazards Not Otherwise Classified]{.HTitleText2} {#h-820004 .Subheading}

#### [Definition]{.HTitleText3} {#h-820005 .Subheading}

Definition of [[health hazard not otherwise
classified]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

**[[8.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.12 .sectionLabel}** In this Subpart,
[[health hazard not otherwise classified]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
health hazard presented by a mixture or substance that is different from
any other health hazard addressed by any other Subpart in this Part and
that has the characteristic of occurring via acute or repeated exposure
and having an adverse effect on the health of a person exposed to it,
including an injury, or resulting in the death of that person.

#### [Classification in the Category of the Class]{.HTitleText3} {#h-820008 .Subheading}

##### [Classification of Substances]{.HTitleText4} {#h-820009 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Category

**[[8.12.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.12.1 .sectionLabel}** A substance that
presents a health hazard not otherwise classified is classified in the
category of this hazard class in accordance with the following table:

<figure id="820012">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom"></th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Category</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Criteria</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight top">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight top">Health Hazards Not Otherwise Classified —
Category 1</td>
<td class="top">A substance that presents a health hazard not otherwise
classified</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>TABLE</p></figcaption>
</figure>

##### [Classification of Mixtures]{.HTitleText4} {#h-820018 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of provisions

**[[8.12.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.12.2 .sectionLabel}** The
classification of a mixture as a health hazard not otherwise classified
in the category of this hazard class must proceed in accordance with the
order of sections 8.12.3 and 8.12.4.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for mixture as a whole

**[[8.12.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.12.3 .sectionLabel}** If data of the
types referred to in subparagraphs 2.1(a)(i) to (iv) are available for
the mixture as a whole, the mixture must be classified as a health
hazard not otherwise classified in accordance with section 8.12.1.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Data available for ingredients

**[[8.12.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.12.4 .sectionLabel}** A mixture that
contains one or more ingredients that are classified as a health hazard
not otherwise classified at a concentration equal to or greater than the
concentration limit of 1.0% is classified in the category "Health
Hazards Not Otherwise Classified --- Category 1".

### [Transitional Provisions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-820042 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this section.

  [[controlled product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[controlled product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in section 2 of the [Hazardous Products Act](/eng/acts/H-3) as it
      read immediately before the day on which subsection 111(1) of the
      [Economic Action Plan 2014 Act, No. 1](/eng/acts/E-0.8) comes into
      force. ([produit contrôlé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[former Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[former Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Controlled
      Products Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-88-66) and the
      [Ingredient Disclosure List](/eng/regulations/SOR-88-64) as they
      each read immediately before the day on which these Regulations
      come into force. ([règlements antérieurs]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance --- supplier

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations do not apply to a supplier in
  respect of the sale or importation of a controlled product that is a
  hazardous product, as defined in section 2 of the [Hazardous Products
  Act](/eng/acts/H-3) as enacted by subsection 111(3) of the [Economic
  Action Plan 2014 Act, No. 1](/eng/acts/E-0.8), if the supplier sells
  or imports the controlled product on or after the first day on which
  both sections 114 and 115 of the [Economic Action Plan 2014 Act, No.
  1](/eng/acts/E-0.8) are in force, but before a day to be fixed by
  order of the Governor in Council for the purposes of sections 130, 133
  and 135 of the [Economic Action Plan 2014 Act, No.
  1](/eng/acts/E-0.8), and if the supplier would not, were the former
  Regulations in force at the time, be in contravention of the former
  Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hazardous product that is not
  controlled product

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations do not apply to a supplier in
  respect of the sale or importation of a hazardous product, as defined
  in section 2 of the [Hazardous Products Act](/eng/acts/H-3) as enacted
  by subsection 111(3) of the [Economic Action Plan 2014 Act, No.
  1](/eng/acts/E-0.8), that is not a controlled product, if the supplier
  sells or imports the hazardous product on or after the first day on
  which both sections 114 and 115 of the [Economic Action Plan 2014 Act,
  No. 1](/eng/acts/E-0.8) are in force, but before a day to be fixed by
  order of the Governor in Council for the purposes of section 130 of
  the [Economic Action Plan 2014 Act, No. 1](/eng/acts/E-0.8).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance --- supplier

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations do not apply to a supplier to whom
  a controlled product that is a hazardous product, as defined in
  section 2 of the [Hazardous Products Act](/eng/acts/H-3) as enacted by
  subsection 111(3) of the [Economic Action Plan 2014 Act, No.
  1](/eng/acts/E-0.8), was sold if the supplier sells the controlled
  product on or after the first day on which both sections 114 and 115
  of the [Economic Action Plan 2014 Act, No. 1](/eng/acts/E-0.8) are in
  force, but before a day to be fixed by order of the Governor in
  Council for the purposes of sections 131, 134 and 136 of the [Economic
  Action Plan 2014 Act, No. 1](/eng/acts/E-0.8), and if the supplier
  would not, were the former Regulations in force at the time, be in
  contravention of the former Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hazardous product that is not
  controlled product

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations do not apply to a supplier to whom
  a hazardous product, as defined in section 2 of the [Hazardous
  Products Act](/eng/acts/H-3) as enacted by subsection 111(3) of the
  [Economic Action Plan 2014 Act, No. 1](/eng/acts/E-0.8), that is not a
  controlled product, was sold if the supplier sells the hazardous
  product on or after the first day on which both sections 114 and 115
  of the [Economic Action Plan 2014 Act, No. 1](/eng/acts/E-0.8) are in
  force, but before a day to be fixed by order of the Governor in
  Council for the purposes of section 131 of the [Economic Action Plan
  2014 Act, No. 1](/eng/acts/E-0.8).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance --- importation --- own use
  in work place

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations do not apply to a supplier in
  respect of the importation of a controlled product that is a hazardous
  product, as defined in section 2 of the [Hazardous Products
  Act](/eng/acts/H-3) as enacted by subsection 111(3) of the [Economic
  Action Plan 2014 Act, No. 1](/eng/acts/E-0.8), if the supplier imports
  the controlled product only for use in their work place on or after
  the first day on which both sections 114 and 115 of the [Economic
  Action Plan 2014 Act, No. 1](/eng/acts/E-0.8) are in force, but before
  a day to be fixed by order of the Governor in Council for the purposes
  of sections 132, 134 and 137 of the [Economic Action Plan 2014 Act,
  No. 1](/eng/acts/E-0.8), and if the supplier would not, were the
  former Regulations in force at the time, be in contravention of the
  former Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hazardous product that is not
  controlled product

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations do not apply to a supplier in
  respect of the importation of a hazardous product, as defined in
  section 2 of the [Hazardous Products Act](/eng/acts/H-3) as enacted by
  subsection 111(3) of the [Economic Action Plan 2014 Act, No.
  1](/eng/acts/E-0.8), that is not a controlled product, if the supplier
  imports the hazardous product only for use in their work place on or
  after the first day on which both sections 114 and 115 of the
  [Economic Action Plan 2014 Act, No. 1](/eng/acts/E-0.8) are in force,
  but before a day to be fixed by order of the Governor in Council for
  the purposes of section 132 of the [Economic Action Plan 2014 Act, No.
  1](/eng/acts/E-0.8).

## [SCHEDULE 1]{.scheduleLabel}[(Paragraphs 4(1)(a) and (b), subsection 4(2), paragraph 4.1(1)(b) and subsections 5(6), 5.6(2) and (3), 5.7(5) to (10), 5.8(1) and 5.9(1))]{.OriginatingRef} {#h-820068 .scheduleLabel}
:::

<figure id="820069">
<table
class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">
<thead>
<tr class="topdouble">
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom bottom"></th>
<th class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 1</th>
<th class="noBorderBottom top" scope="col">Column 2</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Item</th>
<th class="noBorderRight borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Heading</th>
<th class="borderBottom bottom" scope="col">Specific Information
Elements</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">1</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Identification</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> product identifier;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> other means of
identification;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> recommended use and
restrictions on use;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> initial supplier identifier;
and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> emergency telephone number
and any restrictions on the use of that number, if applicable</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Hazard identification</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> classification of the
hazardous product, namely, the appropriate category or subcategory of
the hazard class identified in Subparts 2 to 18 and 21 of Part 7 or
Subparts 1 to 11 of Part 8, or a name that is its substantive
equivalent, or for Subpart 20 of Part 7 and Subpart 12 of Part 8, the
category of the hazard class or a description of the identified
hazard;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> subject to subsections 3(2)
and (4) to (5) of these Regulations, for each category or subcategory in
which the hazardous product is classified, with the exception of the
categories referred to in paragraphs (b.1) to (b.3), the information
elements, namely, the symbol, signal word, hazard statement and
precautionary statement, that are specified for that category or
subcategory in section 3 of Annex 3 of the GHS. If the required
information element is a symbol, either the name of the symbol or the
symbol itself may be used;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b.1)</span> subject to subsection 3(2)
of these Regulations, in the case of a hazardous product that is
classified in the category “Chemicals Under Pressure – Category 1” or
“Chemicals Under Pressure – Category 2”, the symbol, signal word, hazard
statement and precautionary statement that are specified for that
category in section 3 of Annex 3 of the United Nations document entitled
Globally Harmonized System of Classification and Labelling of Chemicals
(GHS), Eighth Revised Edition. Either the name of the symbol or the
symbol itself may be used;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b.2)</span> subject to subsection 3(2)
of these Regulations, in the case of a hazardous product that is
classified in the category “Chemicals Under Pressure – Category 3”, the
symbol, signal word and precautionary statement that are specified for
that category in section 3 of Annex 3 of the United Nations document
entitled Globally Harmonized System of Classification and Labelling of
Chemicals (GHS), Eighth Revised Edition, as well as the hazard statement
“Chemical under pressure: May explode if heated/<span class="otherLang"
lang="fr">Produit chimique sous pression : peut exploser sous l’effet de
la chaleur</span>”. Either the name of the symbol or the symbol itself
may be used;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b.3)</span> subject to subsection 3(2)
of these Regulations, for each category set out in Subparts 17, 18 and
20 of Part 7 and in Subparts 11 and 12 of Part 8 in which the hazardous
product is classified,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> the information elements that
are specified for that category in Schedule 5 (if the required
information element is a symbol, either the name of the symbol or the
symbol itself may be used), and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(ii)</span> any precautionary statements
that are applicable to the hazardous product in terms of</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(A)</span> general precautionary
statements,</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(B)</span> prevention precautionary
statements,</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(C)</span> response precautionary
statements,</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(D)</span> storage precautionary
statements, and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(E)</span> disposal precautionary
statements;</p></li>
</ul></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b.4)</span> the information elements
referred to in paragraphs 3(1)(e) to (g) of these Regulations;
and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> other hazards known to the
supplier with respect to the hazardous product</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Composition/Information on
ingredients</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(1)</span> In the case of a hazardous
product that is a material or substance,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> its chemical name;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> its common name and
synonyms;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> its CAS registry number and
any unique identifiers; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> the chemical name of the
impurities, stabilizing solvents and stabilizing additives that are
known to the supplier, that individually are classified in any category
or subcategory of a health hazard class and that contribute to the
classification of the material or substance</p></li>
</ul></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(2)</span> In the case of a hazardous
product that is a mixture, for each material or substance in the mixture
that, individually, is classified in any category or subcategory of a
health hazard class and is present above the concentration limit that is
designated for the category or subcategory in which it is classified —
regardless of whether the material or substance contributes to the
classification of the mixture as a hazardous product — or is present in
the mixture at a concentration that results in the mixture being
classified in a category or subcategory of any health hazard class,</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> its chemical name;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> its common name and
synonyms;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> its CAS registry number and
any unique identifiers; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> its concentration</p></li>
</ul></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">4</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">First-aid measures</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> a description of necessary
first-aid measures, subdivided according to the different routes of
exposure (inhalation, ingestion, skin and eye contact);</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> the most important symptoms
and effects, whether acute or delayed; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> an indication of immediate
medical attention and special treatment needed, if necessary</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">5</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Fire-fighting measures</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> suitable and unsuitable
extinguishing media;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> specific hazards arising from
the hazardous product, such as the nature of any hazardous combustion
products; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> special protective equipment
and precautions for fire-fighters</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">6</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Accidental release
measures</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> personal precautions,
protective equipment and emergency procedures; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> methods and materials for
containment and cleaning up</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">7</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Handling and storage</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> precautions for safe
handling; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> conditions for safe storage,
including any incompatibilities</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">8</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Exposure controls/Personal
protection</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> control parameters, including
occupational exposure limit values or biological limit values and the
source of those values;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> appropriate engineering
controls; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> individual protection
measures, such as personal protective equipment</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">9</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Physical and chemical
properties</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> physical state;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> colour;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> odour;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> melting point and freezing
point;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> boiling point or initial
boiling point and boiling range;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> flammability;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> lower and upper explosion
limit or lower and upper flammability limit;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(h)</span> flash point;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(i)</span> auto-ignition
temperature;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(j)</span> decomposition
temperature;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(k)</span> pH;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(l)</span> kinematic viscosity;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(m)</span> solubility;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(n)</span> partition coefficient —
n-octanol/water (logarithmic value);</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(o)</span> vapour pressure;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(p)</span> density and relative
density;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(q)</span> relative vapour density;
and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(r)</span> particle
characteristics</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">10</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Stability and reactivity</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> reactivity;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> chemical stability;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> possibility of hazardous
reactions;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> conditions to avoid,
including static discharge, shock or vibration;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> incompatible materials;
and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> hazardous decomposition
products</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">11</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Toxicological information</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><p>Concise but complete description of the
various toxic health effects and the data used to identify those
effects, including</p>
<ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> information on the likely
routes of exposure (inhalation, ingestion, skin and eye
contact);</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> symptoms related to the
physical, chemical and toxicological characteristics;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> delayed and immediate
effects, and chronic effects from short-term and long-term exposure;
and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> numerical measures of
toxicity, including ATEs</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">12</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Ecological information</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> ecotoxicity (aquatic and
terrestrial, if available);</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> persistence and
degradability;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> bioaccumulative
potential;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> mobility in soil;
and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> other adverse
effects</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">13</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Disposal considerations</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Information on safe handling for disposal and
methods of disposal, including any contaminated packaging</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">14</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Transport information</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(a)</span> UN number;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> United Nations proper
shipping name as provided for in the United Nations Model
Regulations;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> transport hazard class as
provided in the United Nations Model Regulations;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(d)</span> packing group as provided in
the United Nations Model Regulations;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(e)</span> environmental hazards
according to the International Maritime Dangerous Goods Code and the
United Nations Model Regulations; and</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> <span
class="Repealed">[Repealed, SOR/2022-272, s. 59]</span></p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(g)</span> special precautions in
connection with transport or conveyance either within or outside the
premises</p></li>
</ul></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">15</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Regulatory information</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom">Safety, health and environmental regulations,
made within or outside Canada, specific to the product in question</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight">16</td>
<td class="noBorderRight">Other information</td>
<td>Date of the latest revision of the safety data sheet</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<figcaption><p>Information Elements on Safety Data
Sheet</p></figcaption>
</figure>

<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(f)</span> incubation period;

</ul></td>

<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">4</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">Section IV — Stability and
Viability</td>
<td class="noBorderBottom"><ul>

susceptibility/resistance;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(b)</span> susceptibility to
disinfectants;</p></li>
<li><p><span class="tablelabel">(c)</span> physical inactivation;

<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">5</td>

<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">6</td>

<tr>

</div>
</figure></td>
<td class="noBorderBottom aligncenter"><figure>

<img src="/images/SOR-2015-17img/SOR-2015-17_ef005.jpg"

This image rests above a horizontal black line of the same width as the black contour of the flame. This pictogram is used to warn about the presence of a flammability hazard." />
</div>
</figure></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">2</td>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom" scope="row">Flame over

This symbol is used to warn about the presence of an oxidizing hazard." />
</div>
</figure></td>
<td class="noBorderBottom aligncenter"><figure>
<div class="imageGroupcenter">
<img src="/images/SOR-2015-17img/SOR-2015-17_ef007.jpg"

This image rests along the contour of the upper half of a black ring with a white centre, the bottom of which rests on a horizontal black line of the same width as the ring diameter. </div>

<tr>
<td class="noBorderRight noBorderBottom">3</td>

<img src="/images/SOR-2015-17img/SOR-2015-17_ef009.jpg"

</figure></td>
</tr>
<tr>

The images of the test tubes have a black outline and white background and include the images of a black liquid near their pour points. The images of the test tubes have a black outline and white background and include the images of a black liquid near their pour points. :::: section
::: ScheduleRP
## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- SOR/2022-272, s. 64

  - - [64]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[former
      Regulations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Hazardous Products
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-17) as they read
      immediately before the day on which these Regulations come into
      force.

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} A supplier may

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} sell or import a hazardous product in
        accordance with the former Regulations; and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} classify a product, mixture, material or
        substance in accordance with the former Regulations if they do
        so using a category or subcategory of a hazard class listed in
        Schedule 2 to the [Hazardous Products Act](/eng/acts/H-3) as it
        read immediately before that day on which these Regulations come
        into force.

    - [(3)]{.lawlabel} This section ceases to have effect on the day
      that is the third anniversary of the day on which these
      Regulations come into force.
:::
